Test source
selection/fire-selectionchange-event-on-deleting-single-character-inside-inline-element.html

Exempt from the 0.1 gate until a renderer answers geometry

each subtest clicks a non-empty inline editing host and presses End before Backspace: the click's caret is the renderer's position-for-point (Blink SelectionController / PositionForPoint) and End's target is the line's end (Blink MoveToEndOfLine, Selection.modify lineboundary, a line-box read); with no renderer neither places a caret, so Backspace deletes nothing and no selectionchange fires (Crane test crane/ed5-delete-at-renderer-caret.html: with the caret at the line's end all three cases pass)
